dates are real `YYYY-MM-DD` calendar dates; violations exit 3 |\n\n### Feature: Usage and Exit Codes\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Usage errors exit 2 | Unknown command, unknown table, unknown report, or wrong argument count exits 2 with diagnostics on stderr only |\n| CLI-002 | Operational failures exit 1 | Unreachable database, missing tables, or unreadable file exits 1 |\n\n### Feature: Test Harness\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| TEST-001 | Provide the automated test suite | `bin/test.sh` runs POSIX-compatible automated tests against a real temporary database and exits zero only when every test passes |\n\n## Surfaced Acceptance Criteria\n\n| ID | Story ID | Criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC-001 | SCHEMA-001 | `ledger init` writes nothing to standard output and exits 0 |\n| AC-002 | LOAD-001 | A rejected load exits 3 and writes nothing to standard output |\n| AC-003 | ACCT-001 | Output amounts are written with exactly two decimal places and no thousands separator |\n| AC-004 | TXN-001 | A posted transaction's `txn_id` is one more than the largest existing id, or 1 when the table is empty |\n| AC-005 | XFER-001 | A transfer stores both rows or neither |\n| AC-006 | RPT-002 | Categories with no transactions appear with zero counts and zero amounts |\n| AC-007 | RPT-003 | Months appear only when they have transactions |\n| AC-008 | CLI-001 | Standard error is written as one diagnostic line on every failing command |\n| AC-009 | TXN-004 | Deleting a transaction is refused when the resulting balance would be negative |\n\n## Relationship Model\n\n| Source or group | Relationship type | Related source or group | Evidence | Delivery implication |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `sources/run.sh` | instruction-to-test | `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` | `run.sh` header: \"exit status is the sole acceptance verdict\"; receipts and payments against cash accounts such as drawers, a safe, and petty cash. It produces balance, category, and monthly reports. Operators run it from a terminal, and every command either completes in full or changes nothing.\n\n## Constraints\n- Python 3.11 or newer, over SQLite, reached only through the shipped persistence library.\n- Amounts are stored as integer cents, never floating point.\n- Database selected by `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`.\n- Output is CSV with `\\n` line endings, no quoting, no trailing spaces.\n- Exit codes 0 through 4 as defined in the sources.\n- Executable named `ledger` at the application root.\n\n## Guardrails\n- A rejected command must change nothing; check every rule before the first write.\n- No command may leave any account balance below zero.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` and never embeds a report answer.\n- Application code never opens a database driver directly.\n- Standard error is diagnostic only and is never compared by the grader.\n- Never modify the read-only scoring assets in `sources/`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT discovery-identity.json ===\n{\n  \"id\": \"discovery-identity\",\n  \"title\": \"Discovery: Project Identity\",\n  \"purpose\": \"Confirm the proposed display name and short description before planning.\",\n  \"questions\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"display_name\",\n      \"label\": \"Display Name\",\n      \"prompt\": \"The display name Drydock will use for this project.
